Playlist AI · 26 September 2026

Every returned song, with its evidence

Both original 40-prompt runs are complete. This report retains all 280 returned songs, their web judgments and the unfilled slots. Open a prompt, then a song to inspect its reasoning, recording caveats and sources.

These are AI web assessments. No audio audition or human listening grades were assigned. The frozen treatment did not improve observed quality or useful coverage. Different seeds and five-minute versus ten-minute budgets prevent a causal comparison. Matched controls and later implementation checks are reported separately in the full audit.

Good: credible scoped evidence supports the request. Partial: related, with central requirements or version details unverified. Mismatch: credible evidence conflicts with the request. Unknown: evidence is insufficient or ambiguous. A good musical match does not imply an independently fingerprinted master.

280 returned songs across 40 prompts. Missing slots remain in case totals.

No returned songs match these filters. Reset filters to see every case, including empty playlists.
